Experiments across LLMs and benchmarks demonstrate improvements in acceptance rate and speedup over state-of-the-art dynamic tree methods. This framework may provide a template for analyzing stochastic tree structures.

</details>


### [131] [Reusing Latent Speech Representations for Query-Conditioned Topic Localization in Transcripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21844)
*Steffen Freisinger,Philipp Seeberger,Thomas Ranzenberger,Tobias Bocklet,Korbinian Riedhammer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究查询条件式话题定位任务，即预测长语音转写文本中与话题标题查询最匹配的句子跨度。方法上复用ASR编码器状态作为句子级表示，并与文本嵌入融合，使轻量级跨度定位器无需额外音频编码器即可利用语音信息。在两个公开数据集上的实验表明，该方法相较于纯文本基线在严格边界匹配标准下持续取得显著提升；跨数据集实验进一步显示，该方法的优势在结构化或半结构化语音中最强，而在自发语音上的增益有限且不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长语音转写文本对下游NLP系统而言是昂贵的输入，且往往包含大量无关上下文。为此，作者研究查询条件式话题定位任务，旨在从长语音转写文本中精准定位与话题标题最相关的句子跨度，以减少下游系统的处理成本并提升效率。

Method: 提出一种融合语音与文本信息的方法：复用自动语音识别（ASR）编码器的中间状态作为句子级语音表示，并将其与文本嵌入进行融合。这种设计允许轻量级的跨度定位器直接利用语音声学信息，而无需额外运行独立的音频编码器，从而在提升定位精度的同时保持计算开销可控。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上的实验显示，融合方法相比纯文本基线在跨度定位上持续获得性能提升，尤其在严格的边界匹配评估标准下优势更为明显。跨数据集实验进一步表明，该方法的收益在结构化或半结构化语音场景中最显著，而在自发语音场景中的增益有限且结果不一致。

Conclusion: 复用ASR编码器状态融合文本嵌入是一种有效的轻量级语音-文本跨模态表示方案，能够显著提升长语音转写文本中的话题跨度定位性能。但该方法的表现受语音类型影响，对结构化/半结构化语音最为有效，对自发语音的适应性仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: Long transcripts are costly inputs for downstream NLP systems and often contain irrelevant context. We study query-conditioned topic localization: predicting the sentence span in a transcript that best addresses a topic-title query. To improve span localization, we reuse ASR encoder states as sentence-level representations and fuse them with textual embeddings. This lets lightweight span locators exploit speech information without running a separate audio encoder. Experiments on two public datasets show consistent gains over text-only baselines, especially under strict boundary-matching criteria. Cross-dataset experiments further indicate that the benefits are strongest for structured or semi-structured speech, while gains on spontaneous speech are limited and mixed.

</details>


### [132] [Do Personality-Tuned LLMs Make Better Social Agents?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21857)
*Tim Krabbe,Xiaodan Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究通过人格感知微调是否能提升大型语言模型在社会模拟中的角色扮演一致性。作者微调了两个小型开源LLM（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Ministral-8B-Instruct），使用人格标注的社交媒体帖子和对话语料构建人格对话引擎。结果显示微调模型在角色扮演不同人格方面并不优于基线模型，且低评分者间一致性限制了结论的可信度；不过微调提升了Qwen模型的词汇多样性。整体上基线模型表现最佳，未来研究应更重视训练数据的质量与领域对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地被用于社会模拟中的社交互动代理和机器人，相比规则系统更灵活。然而尽管它们能很好地模仿人类行为，仍存在持续的"异质性"（alienness）。本研究的动机是探究人格感知微调能否通过提升人格条件对话生成的一致性和可控性（相对于仅用指令提示）来缩小这一差距。

Method: 作者使用结合人格标注的社交媒体帖子和对话的语料，对Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Ministral-8B-Instruct两个小型开源LLM进行微调，构建用于社会模拟的人格对话引擎。随后在多个社交互动场景中评估生成的模型，使用三个独立的LLM评判器评估人格保真度并提供基于证据的行为解释，同时量化评分者间一致性（inter-rater agreement）及生成对话的词汇特征。

Result: 结果表明微调模型在模拟不同人格方面并不优于各自的基线模型。此外，低评分者间一致性限制了结果解读的置信度。在生成文本质量方面，微调模型大多与基线相当，其中微调提升了Qwen模型的词汇多样性。总体结果大致可用，基线模型整体表现最佳。

Conclusion: 尽管当前结果显示出模型具备可用性，但未来研究应更强调训练数据的质量和领域对齐，以准确实现人格角色扮演。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly used in social simulations for socially interactive agents and robots, offering more flexibility than rule-based systems. However, even though they mimic human behaviour very well, there is a persistent alienness to them. This work investigates whether personality-aware fine-tuning can reduce this gap by improving the consistency and controllability of personality-conditioned dialogue generation compared with instruction prompting alone. We fine-tune two small open-weight LLMs, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and Ministral-8B-Instruct, using a corpus that combines personality-labelled social media posts and dialogues to create a personality-based dialogue engine for social simulation. The resulting models are evaluated across multiple social interaction scenarios using three independent LLM judges, which assess personality fidelity and provide evidence-based behavioral interpretations. We additionally quantify inter-rater agreement and lexical characteristics of the generated dialogue. Results indicate that fine-tuned models are not better at role-playing different personalities than their respective baseline models. However, low inter-rater agreement limits the confidence with which these results can be interpreted. Concerning the quality of generated texts, fine-tuned models are mostly comparable to the baselines, with fine-tuning improving the linguistic diversity of the Qwen models. While the results appear generally usable and the baseline models offer the best overall performance, future studies should place greater emphasis on the quality and domain alignment of training data for accurate personality role-playing.

</details>


### [133] [TrialAtlas: Multi-Agent Research Organization for Clinical Trial Design and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21859)
*Jiacheng Lin,Zifeng Wang,Zheng Chen,Erick Scott,Ziwei Yang,Fanyang Yu,Sheng Zhong,Jimeng Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TrialAtlas，一个记忆增强的多智能体研究组织，用于药物临床开发规划（CDP）。它通过协调文献综述、竞争性试验情报、监管先例分析和整合推理等专业智能体，从历史临床试验和监管结果（包括NDA）中学习，并在新基准TrialAtlasBench（由291封FDA完整回复函构建）上超越最强基线，显著提升试验缺陷检测与技术/监管成功预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近90%进入临床开发的药物最终失败，尽管投入数十亿美元研发资金。制药公司依赖CDP和技术/监管成功概率评估来预判开发风险，但现有决策过程劳动密集且主观，需要临床科学、统计、监管事务和竞争情报等多领域专家协作获取、综合和推理异构证据，效率低下、缺乏客观性。

Method: 提出记忆增强的多智能体研究组织TrialAtlas，模拟人类协作流程，协调专门的智能体负责文献综合、竞争性试验情报、监管先例分析和试验设计/开发风险整合推理。系统从历史临床试验和监管结果（包括先前NDA）中学习以积累开发经验。同时构建TrialAtlasBench评估基准，由291封FDA完整回复函（CRL）构成，涵盖三项任务：试验设计缺陷检测、可操作设计改进推荐、技术/监管成功预测。

Result: TrialAtlas在缺陷检测上取得50.0%的F1分数，比最强基线高6.1个百分点；在技术/监管成功预测上达到85.3%的平衡准确率和84.7%的F1，平衡准确率较最佳基线提升6.7个百分点，Cohen's kappa提升12.0个百分点。专家评估中，86.4%的TrialAtlas生成关注点被判定为有效，优于OpenAI DeepResearch（83.1%）和Gemini DeepResearch（59.3%）。

Conclusion: TrialAtlas证明记忆增强多智能体架构能够有效映射人类CDP协作流程，将历史开发经验融入决策，在真实监管场景中显著提升试验设计缺陷识别与开发成功预测的准确性和可靠性，为制药行业临床开发规划提供了更客观、可扩展的智能化解决方案。

Abstract: Nearly 90% of drugs entering clinical development ultimately fail, despite billions of dollars in investment. Pharmaceutical companies therefore rely on clinical development planning (CDP) and probability of technical and regulatory success assessment to anticipate development risks, yet these decisions remain labor-intensive and subjective, requiring experts across clinical science, statistics, regulatory affairs, and competitive intelligence to jointly acquire, synthesize, and reason over heterogeneous evidence. Here, we introduce TrialAtlas, a memory-augmented multi-agent research organization for CDP that mirrors this collaborative process by coordinating specialized agents for literature synthesis, competitive trial intelligence, regulatory precedent analysis, and integrated reasoning over trial design and development risk. TrialAtlas further learns from historical clinical trials and regulatory outcomes, including prior New Drug Applications (NDAs), to ground its decisions in accumulated development experience. To evaluate these capabilities in an authentic regulatory setting, we introduce TrialAtlasBench, constructed from 291 FDA Complete Response Letters and spanning three practical tasks: detecting trial design deficiencies, recommending actionable design improvements, and predicting technical and regulatory success. TrialAtlas achieves an F1 score of 50.0% for deficiency detection, outperforming the strongest baseline by 6.1 points, and reaches 85.3% balanced accuracy and 84.7% F1 for prediction of technical and regulatory success, improving over the best baselines by 6.7 points in balanced accuracy and 12.0 points in Cohen's kappa. In expert evaluation, 86.4% of TrialAtlas-generated concerns were judged valid, compared with 83.1% for OpenAI DeepResearch and 59.3% for Gemini DeepResearch.

</details>


### [134] [NemotronLabs VoiceChat: An Open Full-duplex Speech-to-Speech Model with Tool Calling Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21967)
*Jagadeesh Balam,Travis Bartley,Edresson Casanova,Sanjay Chauhan,Chen Chen,Zhehuai Chen,Zijia Chen,Francesco Ciannella,Slyne Deng,Mikyas Desta,Harishchandra Dubey,Slim Essid,Nourchene Ferchichi,Boris Ginsburg,Mariana Graterol Fuenmayor,Negar Habibi,Kevin Hu,Anand Joseph,Viraj Karandikar,Myungjong Kim,Viacheslav Klimkov,Seelan Lakshmi Narasimhan,Lily Lee,Jason Li,Eileen Long,Ameya Mahabaleshwarkar,Aditya Malte,Adi Margolin,Sasha Meister,Valentin Mendelev,Oluwatobi Olabiyi,Ankita Pasad,Yifan Peng,Elena Rastorgueva,Jayda Ritchie,Jason Roche,Nikhil Srihari,Yuanhang Su,Yoshi Suhara,Viet Anh Trinh,Jinhan Wang,Piotr Zelasko,Hui Wang,Puhui Meng,Chaosen Zhang,Yunsheng Liu,Shawn Wang,Wenjing Li,Zhonglei He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NemotronLabs VoiceChat 是一个开源的、原生支持工具调用的全双工语音到语音（S2S）模型，通过将流式语音编码器、仅解码器语言模型、辅助RNN-T转写分支和流式TTS解码器整合到统一流式架构中，实现"边听边转写、边推理、边调用工具、边说话"的实时对话能力。在多项全双工基准测试中展现出行业领先的停顿处理和打断响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放式语音对话系统大多仅支持半双工（即用户说完后机器才回应），缺乏真正的实时全双工交互能力，难以处理用户插话、背通道反馈等自然对话场景，也难以在对话过程中无缝调用外部工具。作者旨在构建一个能同时完成全双工交互、语音识别与生成、通用语言理解以及外部工具调用，且不牺牲实时对话行为的单一开放模型。

Method: 模型采用统一的流式架构，包含：流式语音编码器、仅解码器语言模型、用于智能体文本与结构化函数调用的并行专用输出流、用于增量用户转写的辅助RNN-T分支，以及流式TTS解码器。这些组件协同工作，使模型能够并行完成聆听、转写、推理、工具调用与语音生成，同时保持自然对话所需的时序行为。

Result: 在Full-Duplex-Bench 1.0上，取得已评估开源系统中最低的停顿处理接管率，用户打断时100%接管，打断后响应质量评分4.33/5；在Full-Duplex-Bench 1.5上，用户背通道反馈后93%案例可恢复响应；在VoiceBench上获得55.1归一化平均分；在Full-Duplex-Bench 3.0上工具选择F1达82.5%。其中参数（argument）准确性与端到端工具执行仍需改进。

Conclusion: 研究证明，全双工交互、语音识别与生成、通用语言能力和外部工具调用可以在单一开源语音到语音模型中集成，同时保持实时对话行为。但工具参数精度与端到端工具执行是后续优化的重点方向。

Abstract: We introduce NemotronLabs VoiceChat, an open full-duplex speech-to-speech model with native tool-calling capabilities. NemotronLabs VoiceChat combines a streaming speech encoder and decoder-only language model with parallel specialized output streams for agent text and structured function calls, an auxiliary RNN-T branch for incremental user transcription, and a streaming TTS decoder. This design enables the model to listen, transcribe, reason, invoke tools, and speak within a unified streaming architecture while preserving the temporal behavior required for natural conversation. On Full-Duplex-Bench 1.0, NemotronLabs VoiceChat achieves the lowest pause-handling takeover rates among evaluated open-weight systems, 100\% takeover following user interruptions, and a 4.33/5 post-interruption response-quality score. On Full-Duplex-Bench 1.5, it resumes its response after user backchannels in 93\% of cases. NemotronLabs VoiceChat obtains a 55.1 normalized average on VoiceBench and, on Full-Duplex-Bench 3.0 (FDB 3.0), achieves 82.5\% tool-selection F1, while argument accuracy and end-to-end tool execution remain areas for improvement. These results demonstrate that full-duplex interaction, speech recognition and generation, general language capabilities, and external tool use can be integrated in a single open speech-to-speech model without sacrificing real-time conversational behavior.

</details>


### [135] [Moral Entropy: Auditing Bias and Uncertainty in Moral Judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21992)
*Maciej Skorski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出道德熵（Moral Entropy）贝叶斯框架，将标注者对道德内容的分歧视为可建模的信息而非噪声，通过保留真实标签的后验分布并将其熵分解为偶然不确定性（不可约分歧）与认知不确定性（标注不足/噪声），同时对任意启发式共识规则进行校准审计。在三个语料库、15个话语领域上的实验表明，标准聚合规则存在系统性偏差：any-annotator规则与校准后验有约30%的项目不一致（几乎全为假阳性，但基础层错误反转），而多数投票和两票规则漏掉63-83%的真阳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算伦理学工作将标注分歧当作噪声丢弃——要么简化为多数投票，要么采用更宽松的any-annotator规则（只要一个标注者标记即通过）。作者认为这种不确定性应当被显式建模和学习，而非投票消解，以便发现标准聚合规则中现有流程无法报告的系统性偏差。

Method: 提出Moral Entropy框架：构建贝叶斯框架保留真实标签的完整后验分布，将其熵分解为偶然不确定性（关于道德内容的不可约分歧）和认知不确定性（由标注不足或噪声引起）；并利用交叉熵/KL、Brier分数和期望校准误差等熵方法，让任何启发式共识规则可对照校准后的真实标签进行审计。

Result: 在三个语料库和十五个话语领域上，审计标准聚合规则发现：any-annotator规则与校准后验在约30%项目上不一致，池化后几乎全部是假阳性（但在基础层MFTC上误差反转，平均FPR/FNR为19.9%/38.9%）；而更严格的多数投票和两票规则漏掉63-83%的真阳性。

Conclusion: 标注分歧应被建模和学习而非投票消解；常用聚合规则（宽松的any-annotator与严格的多数/两票投票）都存在系统性偏差，前者倾向假阳性而后者错过大量真阳性，说明需要以校准后验为基准审计现有启发式共识规则。

Abstract: Most work in computational ethics treats annotator disagreement on moral content as noise to be voted away, collapsed into majority vote or the more permissive any-annotator rule the moment a single annotator flags an item. We argue this uncertainty should instead be modeled and learned from.
  We introduce Moral Entropy, a Bayesian framework that keeps a full posterior over the true label and decomposes its entropy into aleatoric uncertainty (irreducible disagreement about the moral content) and epistemic uncertainty (from insufficient or noisy annotation) -- and lets any heuristic consensus rule be audited against a calibrated ground truth via entropy methods such as cross-entropy/KL, Brier score, and expected calibration error.
  Across three corpora and fifteen discourse domains, auditing the standard aggregation rules against this posterior reveals bias that no current pipeline reports: the any-annotator rule disagrees with the calibrated posterior on roughly 30% of items -- pooled, almost entirely false positives, though the errors invert at the foundation level (19.9%/38.9% mean FPR/FNR on MFTC) -- while the stricter majority and two-vote rules miss 63-83% of true positives.

</details>


### [136] [RecreationWorld: Scalable and Verifiable Environments for Hybrid Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22000)
*Shuai Bai,Jiayong Deng,Yikun Fu,Chang Gao,Xuhao Hu,Mianqiu Huang,Yizhen Jiang,Yuheng Jing,Dehui Kong,Keliang Li,Ning Li,Wanli Li,Dayiheng Liu,Dunjie Lu,Changwei Luo,Que Shen,Zheyuan Wang,Zijian Wang,Jie Wu,Gao Wu,Zhihui Xie,Rui Xie,Haiyang Xu,An Yang,Jiakang Yuan,Yanming Zhang,Jiajun Zhang,Xi Zhang,Zhenru Zhang,Zhuo Zhen,Mingkang Zhu,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究混合计算机使用智能体（CUAs），这类智能体需自主决定探索界面、编写代码并运行验证。作者提出RecreationWorld框架，基于娱乐任务（给定运行参考，构建忠实实现）生成轨迹并训练模型。引入RecreationBench基准（250个跨平台任务），评估显示GPT-6 Astra总体达58.1%，但仅2.8%任务通过全部程序测试；生成应用偏小且单块化。已发布基准、环境和测试套件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体（CUAs）沿图形交互和软件开发两个方向独立发展，但真实数字工作需两者交错结合。目前缺乏同时支持界面探索、代码实现与运行验证的混合CUA研究，且缺少可扩展、可复现的训练与评估环境。

Method: 提出RecreationWorld框架，包含Ubuntu、macOS、Windows、Android和Web五平台环境，提供统一测试工具（原生GUI控制与编码工具）。以运行参考应用作为行为的oracle，用于隐藏行为测试的执行级奖励。利用高质量开源应用大规模生成轨迹并训练模型。引入RecreationBench，含250个多样化任务，程序化与视觉断言覆盖多交互深度，经参考验证与人工审核后冻结自动评分。

Result: 训练模型在五个分布外编码与混合计算机使用基准上性能提升，且更频繁验证渲染输出。GPT-6 Astra在RecreationBench上总体为58.1%，但仅2.8%任务通过全部程序化测试。静态界面结构复现优于交互与计算输出，生成应用规模更小且更单块化。

Conclusion: 混合CUA需同时具备界面探索与代码实现能力，RecreationWorld和RecreationBench可支撑该方向研究。当前模型在忠实复现交互与动态输出方面仍有明显短板，生成应用结构尚不够模块化，提示后续工作需强化交互级验证与软件工程能力。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) have advanced along two separate lines: graphical interaction and software development through code and the command line. Real digital work requires both, interleaved rather than stacked end to end. We study hybrid CUAs that autonomously decide when to explore an interface, implement software, and run and visually verify their artifacts. We introduce RecreationWorld, a five-platform framework built around recreation: given a running reference, an agent must discover its behavior and build a faithful implementation with no prescribed workflow. RecreationWorld provides reproducible environments on Ubuntu, macOS, Windows, Android, and Web, plus a unified harness with native GUI control and coding tools. The running reference serves as an oracle for hidden behavioral tests, providing execution-grounded rewards. We scale trajectory generation with high-quality open-source applications. Models trained on these trajectories improve across five out-of-distribution coding and hybrid computer-use benchmarks and more frequently verify their rendered outputs, providing evidence of transfer beyond recreation. For held-out evaluation, we introduce RecreationBench, comprising 250 diverse tasks across domains and platforms. Reference-grounded programmatic and visual assertions cover action-conditioned outcomes at multiple interaction depths; each is validated on the reference and by human reviewers before the suite is frozen for automatic scoring. GPT-6 Astra leads at 58.1% overall, but passes all programmatic tests on just 2.8% of tasks. Agents reproduce static interface structure more reliably than interactions and computed outputs, while generated applications remain smaller and more monolithic than their references. We release the benchmark, environments, and test suites.

</details>


### [137] [DiaVLo: Diagnosing Behaviours of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22008)
*Lorenzo Corti,Jie Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DiaVLo，一种用于视觉语言模型（VLM）的诊断框架。该框架利用人工策展和VLM的生成能力来构建期望行为与观测行为的规范，揭示潜在的不对齐问题，并提供因果估计以识别影响VLM行为的最具影响力的概念。实验在多个开源VLM的分类和生成条件下进行，结果表明DiaVLo生成的行为标签与模型性能相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM依赖在其各子组件之间存储和传递适当信息。验证VLM表现出期望行为、同时避免有害行为，是其可靠部署的核心。然而，目前识别VLM行为的方法仍然稀缺，缺乏系统性的诊断手段。

Method: DiaVLo框架将人工策展与VLM自身的生成能力相结合，构建期望行为和观测行为的规范（specifications），从而发现潜在的不对齐。此外，框架还提供因果估计，用于识别引导VLM行为的最具影响力的概念（concepts）。评估在多个开源VLM上进行，涵盖分类与生成两种条件。

Result: 实验表明DiaVLo生成的行为标签与模型性能相关，并为测量到的性能提供上下文。该框架揭示出清晰对齐与不对齐的行为，同时展现了VLM在感知、组织和优先级排序概念方面的模式。

Conclusion: DiaVLo是一种有效的VLM行为诊断方法，能够揭示期望与实际行为之间的差异，并提供可操作的因果洞察。它有助于理解VLM如何感知、组织和优先化概念，为VLM的可靠部署提供支撑。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) rely on storing and transferring appropriate information across their sub-components. Verifying that the VLMs exhibit desired behaviours, while avoiding harmful ones, is central to their reliable deployment. Yet, methods that identify VLM behaviours remain scarce. We present DiaVLo, a diagnostic framework that leverages human curation and VLMs' generation capabilities to construct specifications of desired and observed VLM behaviours, surfacing potential misalignments. Beyond this, DiaVLo also provides causal estimates to identify the most influential concepts steering VLM behaviours. We evaluate DiaVLo on several open-source VLMs under both classification and generation conditions. Our experiments show that DiaVLo produces behaviour labels that correlate with model performance and provide context for measured performance. DiaVLo surfaced behaviours that are clearly aligned and misaligned, alongside patterns in how VLMs perceive, organise, and prioritise concepts.

</details>


### [138] [QuranicMMLU: A Cognitively-Aware Benchmark for Evaluating Generative AI Solutions on Quranic Linguistic Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22038)
*Rawan El Ghali,Umm Kulsoom,Anas Madkoor,Dima Faris Alsaudi,Roaa Abdelmagid,Roaa Ibrahim,Raghad Mousa,Hamza Aljaji,Abdullah Khanafer,Abdallah Alkanani,Salah Feras Alali,Rawan Khaled Mohamed,Ehsaneddin Asgari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了QuranicMMLU基准，用于在多个语言学复杂度维度上评估生成式AI对古兰经阿拉伯语的理解能力。该基准基于涵盖音系、形态、句法、语义和语用五大支柱的分类体系（31个细分类别），生成980道经人工审核的问题（同时提供开放式和选择题形式），并按布鲁姆认知层级和经文困惑度分层。对12个系统的评测发现：伊斯兰专用模型表现最佳，但所有系统在选择题上的准确率（平均84%）显著高于开放式回答质量（平均60%），两种评分排名高度一致（Kendall's τ=0.73）——选择题会掩盖仅在去除选项后才暴露的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有古兰经基准主要聚焦于通用问答和语义检索，未能探测特定的语言能力，也未按认知需求和经文难度进行分层。这导致无法对模型在古兰经领域的语言学细微差异（如塔吉威德、词根-词型形态、启示背景、章间连贯性等）进行精细评测，因此需要构建一个更严谨、有语言学理论支撑的评估框架。

Method: 构建了五大支柱的古兰经分类体系（音系、形态、句法、语义、语用），共31个叶节点，涵盖从塔吉威德和词根-词型形态到启示背景和章间连贯性等语言现象。针对每个叶节点，按布鲁姆认知层级和经文困惑度分层生成问题；采用LLM作为评审器独立作答并评分，随后将结果路由至人工审核。最终形成980道人工审核问题，每种问题均以开放式和选择题两种形式发布。

Result: 对12个系统进行基准测试：伊斯兰专用模型表现最优。关键发现为所有系统在选择题准确率（平均84%）上显著高于开放式回答质量（平均60%）；两种评分模式下的系统排名高度一致（Kendall's τ=0.73），但选择题评分会掩盖仅在去除选项后暴露出的模型失败。

Conclusion: QuranicMMLU为古兰经领域的阿拉伯语NLP评估提供了一个严谨且基于语言学的框架。研究揭示了选择题格式可能高估模型能力的风险，强调开放式评估对真实反映模型在古兰经阿拉伯语上的表现至关重要，为后续在宗教文本领域设计更可靠的评测方法指明了方向。

Abstract: We introduce QuranicMMLU, a benchmark for evaluating generative AI on Quranic Arabic across multiple dimensions of linguistic complexity. Existing Quranic benchmarks center on general question answering and semantic retrieval, without probing specific linguistic competencies or stratifying by cognitive demand and verse difficulty. We construct a five-pillar Quranic taxonomy spanning Phonology, Morphology, Syntax, Semantics, and Pragmatics, with 31 leaves covering phenomena from tajwīd and root-and-pattern morphology to occasions of revelation and inter-surah coherence. For each leaf we generate questions stratified by Bloom's cognitive level and verse perplexity, then have LLM as a judge to independently answer and score every item and route the annotations to manual review. The resulting dataset comprises 980 human-reviewed questions, each issued in both open-ended and multiple-choice form. We benchmark 12 systems on these items and find that the Islamic-specialized model leads, yet every system scores higher on multiple-choice accuracy (average 84%) than open-ended answer quality (average 60%): the two rankings agree closely (Kendall's τ=0.73), but multiple-choice scoring hides failures that surface only once answer choices are removed. QuranicMMLU thus offers a rigorous, linguistically grounded framework for evaluating Arabic NLP in the Quranic domain.

</details>


### [139] [An Interpretable Memory Decision Controller for LLM Agents Based on Three-Signal Complementarity: Decoupling Confidence and Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22043)
*Yiming Zhang,Jinghong Zhang,Haoran Zhao,Yiren Ma,Chunlei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出记忆决策层（MDL），一个零参数、完全白盒的记忆决策控制器，位于检索与生成阶段之间，通过QR正交子空间投影融合相关性、可靠性与任务风险三信号，显式解耦置信度与一致性，引入风险反转和显式弃权，在冲突记忆场景下将幻觉率降低约56.04%，高风险场景接近零幻觉，每次决策仅耗时约0.14毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向大语言模型的记忆系统主要聚焦于高效检索，却较少关注检索到的记忆是否应被信任这一决策问题。当记忆库包含冲突立场时，标准RAG会盲目注入记忆并放大幻觉——在易受记忆注入影响的模型中，冲突记忆下的RAG幻觉率显著高于无记忆基线。作者从 prefrontal cortex（前额叶皮层）的记忆信号机制中获得灵感，旨在解决冲突记忆下的信任决策难题。

Method: 提出记忆决策层（MDL），核心是一个三信号互补编码器：通过QR-based正交子空间投影将相关性、可靠性和任务风险三信号融合，并加入元工作记忆信号，形成可解释的决策表示来量化检索记忆的可信度。在此基础上，MDL显式地将置信度与一致性解耦，并引入风险反转与显式弃权机制。该控制器完全白盒，仅依赖几何运算，无需训练参数。

Result: 在主流大语言模型和多个开源数据集上的评估显示：MDL在一般场景下将冲突记忆下的幻觉率降低约56.04%，在高风险场景下接近零幻觉；每次决策仅增加约0.14毫秒，比其前置的embedding检索步骤快约50倍，比LLM自评估调用快四到五个数量级。

Conclusion: MDL以零参数、完全白盒的几何运算方式，在不训练模型的前提下显著缓解了冲突记忆对RAG的幻觉放大问题，兼具高有效性与极低延迟，为记忆系统的信任决策提供了一种可解释、可扩展的轻量级控制器方案。

Abstract: Memory systems for large language models have focused predominantly on efficient retrieval, whereas the decision of whether retrieved memories should be trusted has received comparatively little attention. When the memory store contains conflicting positions, standard retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) blindly injects memories and amplifies hallucinations: in models susceptible to memory injection, the RAG hallucination rate under conflicting memories is markedly higher than that of a memory-free baseline. Inspired by memory signaling mechanisms in the prefrontal cortex, we propose the Memory Decision Layer (MDL), a zero-parameter memory decision controller situated between the retrieval and generation stages. Its core is a three-signal complementary encoder that fuses relevance, reliability, and task risk through QR-based orthogonal subspace projection and a meta-working-memory signal into an interpretable decision representation that quantifies the trustworthiness of retrieved memories. Building on this encoder, MDL explicitly decouples confidence from consistency and introduces risk inversion and explicit abstention. Evaluations on mainstream large language models and multiple open-source datasets show that MDL reduces the hallucination rate under conflicting memories by about 56.04% in general scenarios and approaches zero hallucination in high-risk scenarios. The controller is fully white-box: it relies purely on geometric operations, requires no trained parameters, and adds only about 0.14 ms per decision -- roughly 50x faster than the embedding-retrieval step that precedes it and four to five orders of magnitude faster than an LLM self-evaluation call.

</details>


### [140] [Cross-sector generalization of accident-process role classification in occupational accident narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22081)
*Aho Yapi,Pierre Latouche,Arnaud Guillin,Yan Bailly*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了法语职业事故叙述中事故过程角色分类的跨行业泛化能力。基于42,244个来自建筑行业的事实单元训练角色分类器，然后在不进行重训练或目标域调优的情况下，将其应用于冶金、化工-塑料行业以及独立收集的公司语料。任务特定的微调和监督表示学习策略始终优于冻结的预训练表示，三种领先策略在三个目标语料上平均平衡准确率达到85.6%-85.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 职业事故叙述中包含关于工作情境、不利条件、事故事件及其后果的宝贵信息，自动结构化这些叙述可促进大规模事故分析和职业风险预防。然而，不同行业和组织间的事故描述术语和写作风格差异显著，这引发了自动化编码系统能否在其训练领域之外泛化的疑问。因此，评估自动化系统在跨行业场景下的泛化能力至关重要。

Method: 作者构建了专家标注语料库，将事实单元分为四类角色：工作情境(A0)、明确报告的不利条件(A1)、事故事件或偏差(B)、以及报告后果(C)。角色分类器仅在建筑行业6,040篇叙述中提取的42,244个事实单元上开发和选择，然后在未见过的冶金和化工-塑料行业语料以及独立收集的公司语料上进行评估，不进行重训练或目标域调优。比较了冻结的预训练表示、任务特定微调和监督表示学习策略。

Result: 任务特定的适配在所有迁移设置中一致优于冻结表示。在多次重复训练运行中，三种领先的任务适配策略在三个目标语料上实现了85.6%至85.8%的平均平衡准确率。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持开发可迁移的辅助编码系统，该系统能够一致地结构化异构的职业事故叙述，用于专家审查和跨行业预防分析。

Abstract: Occupational accident narratives contain valuable information about work situations, unfavourable conditions, accident events, and their consequences. Automatically structuring these narratives can facilitate large-scale accident analysis and support occupational risk prevention. However, the terminology and writing styles used to describe accidents vary considerably across sectors and organisations, raising questions about the ability of automated coding systems to generalize beyond their training domain. In this paper, we evaluate the cross-sector generalization of accident-process role classification in French occupational accident narratives. We construct an expert-annotated corpus in which factual units are classified into four roles: work situation (A0), explicitly reported unfavourable condition (A1), accident event or deviation (B), and reported consequence (C). The role classifiers are developed and selected exclusively on 42,244 factual units extracted from 6,040 construction-sector narratives and are then evaluated on unseen corpora from the metallurgy and chemistry--plastics sectors, as well as on an independently collected company corpus, without retraining or target-domain tuning of the role classifier. We compare frozen pretrained representations with task-specific fine-tuning and supervised representation-learning strategies. The results show that task-specific adaptation consistently improves cross-domain transfer over frozen representations. Across repeated training runs, the three leading task-adapted strategies achieved average balanced accuracies between 85.6% and 85.8% across the three target corpora. These findings support the development of transferable assisted-coding systems capable of consistently structuring heterogeneous occupational accident narratives for expert review and cross-sector prevention analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Sparse Priors for Efficient Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20883)
*Saumya Goyal,Barnabás Póczos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"稀疏先验"（sparse priors）及"稀疏维度"（Sparse Dimension）概念，用于刻画真实应用中分布的结构性稀疏特征。作者证明在k-稀疏先验下分布学习的贝叶斯风险下界为Ω(√(k/n))，并在温和附加假设下给出TV距离下匹配（至多对数项）的上界，从而克服了传统O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})维度灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分布学习的理论保证受维度灾难影响，其误差界为O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})且已被证明是最小最大最优的。但作者认为这一结果过于悲观，因为仅依赖光滑性假设不足以捕捉真实应用中分布的结构。为此需要引入能够反映分布内在稀疏结构的假设类，以突破维度灾难。

Method: 作者定义稀疏先验类和"稀疏维度"k，作为对分布空间中先验稀疏性的度量。在贝叶斯框架下，推导了k-稀疏先验下分布学习的风险下界Ω(√(k/n))，并证明在温和附加假设下TV距离存在匹配上界（对数项内渐近紧）。同时证明了贝叶斯设定下分布学习与"学习生成采样"的统计等价性，使结果适用于生成式采样任务。

Result: 分布学习在k-稀疏先验下达到贝叶斯风险下界Ω(√(k/n))，并给出TV距离下匹配（至多为对数因子）的上界。证明了分布学习与学习采样的统计等价性。尽管k可能仍依赖维度d或内在维度概念，结果表明在适当的先验下学习可以克服关于n的维度诅咒。

Conclusion: 通过在传统分布学习中引入稀疏先验结构，可以显著改善样本复杂度：学习率从指数依赖维度的O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})提升到仅依赖稀疏度k的O(√(k/n))。这一结果也推广到生成采样任务，说明在合适的先验假设下，生成式AI的理论保证可以突破维度灾难的限制。

Abstract: Despite the widespread use and success of generative AI techniques today, theoretical guarantees on learning a distribution supported in $d$ dimensions from $n$ samples degrade as $O(n^{-1/Θ(d)})$, though shown to be minimax optimal. We hypothesize that present bounds are too pessimistic because smoothness assumptions are not enough to capture the structure of distributions that often appear in real applications. Consequently, we introduce the class of sparse priors and define the "Sparse Dimension" as a measure of sparsity of a prior over the space of all distributions. We show that distribution learning under a $k$-sparse prior achieves a Bayesian risk lower bound of $Ω(\sqrt{k/n})$ under common distance metrics, and show a matching (up to logarithmic terms asymptotically in $n,k$) upper bound for the TV distance under mild additional assumptions. We show the statistical equivalence of distribution learning and learning to sample in the Bayesian setting so that our results apply to learning to sample as well. While $k$ can still depend on the dimension $d$, or a notion of intrinsic dimension, our results show that learning under an appropriate prior overcomes the curse of dimensionality with respect to the dependence on $n$.

</details>


### [142] [BI-Agent and BI-Bench: Towards Automating End-to-End Business Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20886)
*Chuxuan Hu,Yeye He,Penny Zhou,Wee Hyong Tok,Daniel Kang,Surajit Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了端到端商业智能（BI）基准 BI-Bench，从真实 BI 项目中提取问答对，系统评估 LLM 在 BI 任务上的能力；发现现有大模型准确率不足 50%，并提出工具增强的 BI-Agent（将 BI 工作流分解为搜索、连接、转换等子任务）结合后训练（SFT + RL）框架，使准确率提升最高达 40 个百分点，验证了工具增强推理与领域专用后训练结合的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 BI 工作流要求用户手动完成数据准备（识别相关表、数据变换、构建连接关系）后才能回答问题，过程复杂且耗时。鉴于 LLM 在数据处理上的强大能力，作者希望研究其能否端到端地直接回答 BI 问题，而无需用户手动完成繁琐的准备步骤，以降低 BI 使用门槛。

Method: 1) 从公开来源收集大量真实 BI 项目，人工从真实用户仪表盘中提取（问题、真实答案）对，构建首个系统性研究端到端 BI 的基准 BI-Bench。2) 设计工具增强的 BI-Agent，将 BI 工作流分解为结构化数据上的子任务（搜索、连接、变换等），并编排专门的数据管理方法。3) 开发后训练框架，从真实 BI 项目中合成训练轨迹，对 BI-Agent 采用 SFT 和 RL 进行后训练。

Result: 前沿 LLM 在 BI-Bench 上表现不佳（准确率低于 50%）。BI-Agent 在普通 LLM 基础上带来最高 40 个百分点的准确率提升；经后训练的 BI-Agent 进一步带来最高 30 个百分点的提升。

Conclusion: 工具增强的推理与领域专用后训练的结合在复杂 BI 工作流中具有重要意义，实验结果指出了未来研究的有前景方向。

Abstract: Business intelligence (BI) is a cornerstone of enterprise decision-making and is widely used by enterprise users in software such as Power BI and Tableau. In traditional BI workflows, users need to prepare data by (1) identifying relevant tables, (2) performing data transformations, and (3) building join relationships, before they can (4) answer their business questions. These steps can be complex and time-consuming, making BI challenging.
  Given the strong capabilities of large language models (LLMs) in working with data, we study their ability to answer BI questions end-to-end, without requiring users to manually perform the tedious preparation steps. To do this, we harvest a large collection of real-world BI projects from public sources, and manually extract pairs of (questions, ground-truth answers) from real user dashboards. The resulting benchmark, BI-Bench, is the first benchmark to systematically study LLMs' ability on end-to-end BI.
  We find that even frontier LLMs perform poorly on BI-Bench, with less than 50% accuracy. To address their limitations, we design a tool-augmented BI-Agent that decomposes BI workflows into subtasks on structured data, such as search, join, and transform, and orchestrates specialized data management methods across BI stages. Furthermore, we develop a post-training framework that synthesizes training trajectories from real BI projects, enabling BI-Agent to be further post-trained using both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL). BI-Agent achieves substantial accuracy gains of up to 40 percentage points with vanilla LLMs, and post-trained BI-Agent yields gains of up to 30 points. Our results highlight the importance of combining tool-augmented reasoning with domain-specific post-training in complex BI workflows, and point to promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [143] [Elastic Threshold Attention: Learned Contextual Sparsity for Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20888)
*Themistoklis Haris,Henry Li,Maryam Karimzadehgan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出弹性阈值注意力（ETA），一种端到端可训练的稀疏注意力架构，可在不牺牲稠密模型质量的前提下实现硬件加速的推理速度。ETA直接从查询表示预测动态上下文阈值，通过乘法抑制而非删除方式训练，使模型能智能分配稠密级上下文给困难检索步骤并剪枝常规token，最终在1.45B模型上与稠密注意力媲美，解码时利用Triton自定义内核实现最高2.5倍于FlashAttention-2的解码加速，并额外提供离线校准算法进一步削减27%注意力计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码中，庞大的KV缓存会造成严重的内存带宽瓶颈。现有稀疏注意力方法通过选择性加载来缓解该问题，但依赖刚性启发式规则，会丢弃必要上下文导致质量下降。核心挑战在于如何在保留稠密模型质量的同时获得硬件加速的稀疏解码速度。

Method: ETA采用端到端可训练架构，从查询表示直接预测动态的上下文敏感阈值，使模型对困难检索/推理步骤分配稠密级上下文，对常规token进行剪枝。训练阶段通过乘法抑制子阈值logit到零而非直接删除，避免表示坍塌；这种平滑均匀注意力底层的训练提供了一个分布式概率储备池，消除了初始token上的注意力汇（attention sinks），并支持推理期硬剪枝与吸收粗粒度GPU块选择带来的附带token。推理时，设计了基于Triton的自定义解码内核，利用缓存的几何-概率边界以O(1)时间筛选KV块。

Result: 1.45B预训练ETA模型在语言建模、常识推理与长上下文针检索任务上与稠密注意力相媲美，实现了约85%训练稀疏度和约38%主动解码密度。推理阶段自定义Triton内核在512K token序列上相较于FlashAttention-2实现最高2.5倍墙钟解码加速。离线校准算法通过冻结多头常量阈值消除预测器开销，额外削减27%注意力计算。

Conclusion: ETA证明了可学习的上下文动态阈值注意力能够在高稀疏度下保持稠密模型质量，通过训练阶段的乘法抑制与平滑注意力底层解决了稀疏训练中的表示坍塌和注意力汇问题，同时以O(1)的几何-概率边界筛选实现了显著的硬件解码加速，并提供了面向特定领域部署的离线校准方案，为长上下文高效推理提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Massive KV caches can cause severe memory-bandwidth bottlenecks during long-context decoding. Sparse attention methods mitigate this via selective loading, but that comes at a cost: rigid heuristics drop necessary context, leading to quality degradation. We introduce \textbf{Elastic Threshold Attention (ETA)}, an end-to-end trainable architecture that achieves hardware-accelerated decoding speed without sacrificing dense model quality. ETA predicts dynamic, contextual thresholds directly from query representations, allowing the model to allocate dense-like context to difficult retrieval or reasoning steps while pruning routine tokens. To learn this policy from scratch without representation collapse, ETA \emph{multiplicatively suppresses} sub-threshold logits toward zero during training rather than deleting them. Training against this smooth uniform attention floor provides a distributed probability reservoir that \textbf{causes localized attention sinks on initial tokens to disappear}. It also enables the model to hard-prune uninformative KV blocks at inference time and absorb incidental tokens co-admitted by coarse GPU block selection. As a result, a 1.45B pretrained ETA model rivals dense attention across language modeling, commonsense reasoning, and long-context needle retrieval at $\approx 85\%$ training sparsity and $\approx 38\%$ active decode density. At inference time, we implement a custom decode kernel in Triton that screens KV blocks in $O(1)$ time using cached geometric-probabilistic bounds, delivering up to $2.5\times$ wall-clock decode speedups over FlashAttention-2 on sequences up to 512K tokens. Finally, we introduce an offline calibration algorithm for domain-specific deployments that freezes per-head constant thresholds to eliminate predictor overhead, cutting attention compute by an additional $27\%$.

</details>


### [144] [Bio-MF: Low-Latency and High-Fidelity EEG-to-fNIRS Cross-Modal Generation for Hybrid Motor-Imagery Brain--Computer Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20904)
*Boyuan Zhao,Sifan Zhang,Luping Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Bio-MF，一种无隐变量的单步 MeanFlow 框架，用于 EEG 条件驱动的 fNIRS 生成。通过直接的信号空间 x-prediction 并将其转换为 MeanFlow 速度监督，Bio-MF 仅需一次网络推理即可完成生成，在 RTX PRO 6000 上单次 fNIRS trial 生成仅需 7.0 ms，较 1000 步 SCDM 提升 857 倍。采用空间-时间交互式 4D 编码、跨模态无分类器引导和噪声级门控 FFT 正则化来保持任务相关的血流动力学结构，在多个数据集上使混合 MI 解码 ACC 较 EEG-only 提升 2.50~4.15 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合 MI-BCI 结合 EEG 与 fNIRS 可优于 EEG-only 系统，但实际中配对采集往往不可用或不方便。现有 EEG-to-fNIRS 跨模态生成方法存在生成速度慢、常需预训练的问题，限制实时应用；而一步生成模型虽可实现低延迟合成，但去除迭代精化会降低保真度并引入非生理伪影。因此需在低延迟与生成质量间取得平衡。

Method: 提出 Bio-MF，一种无隐变量的一步 MeanFlow 框架：1) 在信号空间直接进行 x-prediction 预测；2) 将该信号空间输出转换为 MeanFlow 速度监督，以一次网络评估完成推理；3) 集成 Spatial-Temporal Interactive 4D Encoding 以在异构传感器布局下保持任务相关血流动力学结构；4) 采用跨模态无分类器引导；5) 引入噪声级门控 FFT 正则化抑制非生理伪影。

Result: 在 Dataset 1 上，EEG+合成 fNIRS 使 ACC 相对 EEG-only 提升 3.37（HbR）和 4.15（HbO）个百分点；在 Dataset 2 的未见过的 64 通道 EEG 布局下，提升仍达 2.98 和 2.50 个百分点。在 RTX PRO 6000 GPU 上单次 fNIRS trial 生成耗时 7.0 ms，较 1000 步 SCDM 获得 857 倍加速。

Conclusion: Bio-MF 在保持任务相关生成质量的同时，实现了极低延迟的 EEG-to-fNIRS 单步合成，能够满足实时混合 MI 解码的需求，并展现出对未见 EEG 布局的泛化能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: Hybrid motor-imagery brain-computer interfaces (MI-BCIs) combining EEG and fNIRS can outperform EEG-only systems by exploiting complementary electrophysiological and hemodynamic information. To obtain such hybrid information when paired EEG-fNIRS acquisition is unavailable or inconvenient, recent studies have focused on EEG-to-fNIRS cross-modal generation. However, existing methods still suffer from slow generation and often require pretraining, limiting their use in real-time MI-BCI scenarios. Although one-step generative models offer an attractive route to low-latency synthesis, removing the iterative refinement process can reduce generation fidelity and introduce non-physiological artifacts. To address these problems, this paper proposes Bio-MF, a latent-free one-step MeanFlow framework for EEG-conditioned fNIRS generation. Bio-MF performs direct signal-space x-prediction, converts this signal-space output into MeanFlow velocity supervision, and completes inference with one network evaluation. To preserve task-relevant hemodynamic structure under heterogeneous sensor layouts, Bio-MF integrates Spatial-Temporal Interactive 4D Encoding, cross-modal classifier-free guidance, and noise-level-gated FFT regularization. On Dataset 1, EEG + synthetic fNIRS improves ACC over EEG-only by 3.37 and 4.15 percentage points for HbR and HbO, respectively. On Dataset 2, the corresponding gains remain 2.98 and 2.50 percentage points under the unseen 64-channel EEG montage. On an RTX PRO 6000 GPU, Bio-MF generates one fNIRS trial in 7.0 ms, corresponding to an 857x speedup over the 1000-step SCDM latency. These results show that Bio-MF enables fast EEG-to-fNIRS synthesis while preserving task-relevant generation quality for downstream hybrid MI decoding. Our code is available at https://github.com/psychosiwa/Bio-MF.

</details>


### [145] [Continuous Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent and Continuous-Time Reinforcement Learning from History of Astrophysical Time Series Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20906)
*Debartha Paul,Juncheng Yi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文回顾了将随机微分方程（SDE）与神经网络参数化相结合以模拟类星体光变曲线的方法，并提出两种新算法：Continuous-Delayed-Memory SGD（依赖离散迭代过程过去状态的随机梯度下降变体）以及基于连续时间策略梯度的强化学习结构（无需求解HJB偏微分方程即可获得探索性策略）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类星体是由超大质量黑洞驱动的宇宙中高光度天体，其亮度呈现随机波动，这些波动编码了黑洞的关键信息。从地面巡天数据的时间序列（即光变曲线）中建模这些波动具有统计挑战性，论文旨在通过SDE与神经网络参数化结合的方式来克服这一挑战。

Method: 1) 回顾了SDE结合神经网络参数化建模光变曲线的历史方法；2) 提出了Continuous-Delayed-Memory SGD，该算法依赖于离散迭代过程的过去状态；3) 在二维地形上进行模拟，通过调整超参数与Vanilla SGD对比，观察到更广泛的探索行为和更精确的收敛表现；4) 提出了一种带有连续时间策略梯度的强化学习结构，用于探索性策略，无需求解Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman (HJB)偏微分方程，并证明其最优性条件可恢复先前工作中的Gibbs策略。

Result: 在二维景观上的仿真表明，所提出的Continuous-Delayed-Memory SGD相比Vanilla SGD具有更宽的探索范围和更精确的收敛行为；所提出的强化学习结构的最优性条件与先前工作的Gibbs策略一致，验证了其理论正确性。

Conclusion: 论文表明SDE与神经网络的结合为类星体光变曲线建模提供了有效途径，同时所提出的新算法在优化探索与收敛性方面优于基线方法，强化学习框架也在理论上与既有工作兼容。

Abstract: Quasars are luminous objects in the universe that exhibit stochastic brightness variations encoding information about the supermassive black holes powering them, and modeling these variations from ground-based survey data time series, known as light curves, is a statistical challenge. This paper reviews how stochastic differential equations (SDEs) have been adapted with neural network parameterizations to overcome this challenge in history. We create the Continuous-Delayed-Memory Stochastic Gradient Descent which depend on the past state of the discrete iteration process. We performed the simulation on some 2-dimensional landscape and observed some wider-exploration and more precise convergent behavior compared to Vanilla SGD by adjusting hyperparameters. Besides, we proposed a reinforcement learning structure with continuous time policy gradients for exploratory policies without solving HJB PDE, and we show that its optimality conditions recover the Gibbs policy of previous works.

</details>


### [146] [Do Quantum Models Scale Like LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20912)
*David S. Berman,Ying-Jer Kao,Roger G. Melko,Alexander G. Stapleton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究RydbergGPT——一种在里德伯原子阵列量子比特投影测量数据上训练的自回归Transformer模型——的神经缩放定律。研究发现，在量子系统的临界点附近，损失随训练数据规模的幂律缩放关系良好（带损失下限修正），而远离临界点时幂律描述质量显著下降。通过熵归一化、有限样本修正的互信息两点函数对比发现，近临界统计特征与自然语言语料最接近，支持多尺度依赖有助于稳定神经缩放、缩放行为应视为模型-数据对属性的假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究神经网络缩放定律是否单纯由模型架构决定，还是也受训练数据结构的影响。通过量子系统这一可控平台，研究数据统计特性（尤其是临界性/多尺度结构）与神经缩放行为之间的关联。

Method: 1) 训练RydbergGPT自回归Transformer模型于里德伯原子阵列的量子投影测量数据；2) 在不同激光失谐参数（跨越临界点）下拟合损失-数据集规模的幂律关系（含损失下限修正项）；3) 利用熵归一化、有限样本修正的互信息两点函数，对比量子测量数据与自然语言语料的统计结构。

Result: 近临界点处损失随数据集规模呈良好幂律缩放（含损失下限）；远离临界点时幂律拟合质量显著降低。近临界量子数据的互信息两点函数衰减最慢、与自然语言语料最接近，而远离临界点的数据两点函数衰减更快。

Conclusion: 研究支持以下假说：数据中的多尺度依赖结构是稳定神经缩放行为的必要条件之一；神经缩放定律不应仅被视为模型属性，而应被视为模型与数据配对组合所共同决定的属性。

Abstract: In this work, we study the neural scaling laws of RydbergGPT, an autoregressive transformer model trained on qubit projective measurement data gathered from interacting Rydberg atom arrays. The quantum system is known to exhibit a finite-size remnant of a critical point as the laser detuning parameter is varied. We find that near the critical point the transformer loss as a function of training dataset size is well described by a power-law with a loss floor correction. However, away from criticality the quality of the power-law description is substantially reduced. We then compare the statistical structure of both Rydberg measurements and natural-language corpora using an entropy-normalised, finite sample corrected mutual information "two-point" function. We find that near-critical statistics of the two point functions are closest to those observed in natural-language, whilst other qubit configurations far from the critical point have two-point functions that decay more rapidly. This supports the hypothesis that multi-scale dependence contributes to stable neural scaling, and that scaling behaviour should be viewed as a property of the model-data pair.

</details>


### [147] [When AI Reviews Train AI Reviewers: Scientific-Judgment Collapse and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20942)
*Sy-Tuyen Ho,Minghui Liu,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在受控实验中揭示了AI同行评审的递归训练会导致"科学判断坍缩"（scientific-judgment collapse）现象——即使用模型生成评论训练后继审稿模型会压缩评分分布并降低语义多样性。为此，作者提出了TrustReviewer系统，通过训练阶段的语料库干预和测试阶段的激活引导（paired activation steering）来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地参与科学评审，模型生成的评审意见进入公开数据和未来训练语料后，AI同行评审可能变得递归：后来的审稿模型会学习早期模型产生的判断。这种反馈循环可能导致评审质量退化，但目前缺乏对此问题的系统性研究。

Method: 作者以Llama 3.1 8B为基础，先在2018-2023年的官方ICLR评审数据上微调审稿模型，然后在2024年数据上以系统性混合的官方评审和模型生成评审来训练四个后继模型。TrustReviewer采用两阶段干预：（1）训练期预防：使用精心策展的语料库进行单阶段训练以减少低质量和语义退化的监督；（2）测试期校正：通过配对激活引导（paired activation steering）减轻判断坍缩的残余倾向，无需额外训练或专家标注。

Result: 实验发现，引入合成评审会压缩评分分布，并降低同论文层面的语义多样性以及语料库层面的多样性，即"科学判断坍缩"。TrustReviewer通过训练期和测试期的双重干预有效缓解了这一问题，改善了推荐对齐度并保持了判断多样性。

Conclusion: 该研究刻画了递归审稿训练的具体风险，并为AI辅助科学评估中保持判断多样性和改善推荐对齐提供了实用干预方案。这为构建可持续的AI评审系统提供了重要的方法论基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly participate in scientific evaluation, both as automated reviewers and as assistants to human reviewers. As model-generated reviews enter public data and future training corpora, AI peer review can become recursive: later reviewers learn from judgments produced by earlier models. We study one step of this feedback loop in a controlled setting. Starting from Llama 3.1 8B, we first fine-tune a reviewer on official ICLR reviews from 2018--2023 and then train four successor models on ICLR 2024 data with systematically varied mixtures of official and model-generated reviews. Our study shows that introducing synthetic reviews compresses rating distributions and reduces both same-paper and corpus-level semantic diversity. We call this pattern $\textbf{scientific-judgment collapse}$.
  To mitigate this failure mode, we introduce $\textbf{TrustReviewer}$, an open-source LLM-based system for generating peer reviews of AI and machine learning papers. TrustReviewer intervenes at two complementary stages. For training-time prevention, we train the core reviewer in a single stage on a curated corpus designed to reduce low-quality and semantically degenerate supervision. For test-time correction, paired activation steering aims to further mitigate residual tendencies toward collapsed judgments without further training or additional expert annotation. Together, these results characterize a concrete risk of recursive reviewer training and provide practical interventions for preserving judgment diversity and improving recommendation alignment in AI-assisted scientific evaluation.

</details>


### [148] [Efficient Bayes-Adaptive Reinforcement Learning with Temporal Logic Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20954)
*Jonathan Hau,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的基于模型的强化学习（RL）算法，用于在未知环境中高效地合成满足线性时序逻辑（LTL）规范（如安全或可达性）的策略。该方法将LTL任务的极限确定性Büchi自动机（LDBA）表示与环境的贝叶斯自适应马尔可夫决策过程（BAMDP）表示同步，并通过提出的贝叶斯自适应蒙特卡洛规划（BAMCP）算法实现近似贝叶斯最优的策略合成。实验证明了该方法在属性满足和样本效率方面的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的LTL任务RL方法通常采用非贝叶斯方法，在探索与利用的权衡上不够高效，导致样本效率低且任务违规较多。作者旨在通过贝叶斯RL框架结合LDBA和BAMDP，实现更高效的策略合成，并减少训练过程中的任务违规次数。

Method: 1) 将LTL规范转换为LDBA表示，并与环境的BAMDP表示同步构建联合状态空间；2) 提出一种新的贝叶斯自适应蒙特卡洛规划（BAMCP）算法，在同步的BAMDP结构上进行近似贝叶斯最优策略合成；3) 通过增强的探索-利用权衡利用贝叶斯RL的优势。

Result: 在有限和无限时域的任务实验中，该方法在属性满足率和样本效率上均优于传统无模型方法；消融实验验证了提出的BAMCP算法相比经典BAMCP在LTL任务满足上的优势；在谨慎RL场景中成功减少了训练期间的任务违规次数。

Conclusion: 通过将LTL规范与贝叶斯自适应规划相结合，本文的方法提供了一种有效且样本高效的策略合成途径，特别适用于需要安全保证或谨慎行为约束的RL应用场景。

Abstract: We present a novel end-to-end model-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithm for efficient policy synthesis under given Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) specifications (e.g., safety or reachability) in unknown environments. To do so, a Limit-Deterministic B{ü}chi Automaton (LDBA) representation of the LTL task is synchronised with a Bayes-Adaptive Markov Decision Process (BAMDP) representation of the environment, which allows us to leverage an enhanced exploration-exploitation trade-off that is achieved via Bayesian RL, as opposed to traditional non-Bayesian approaches. We further propose a novel Bayes-Adaptive Monte-Carlo Planning (BAMCP) algorithm to allow for approximate Bayes-optimal strategy synthesis in the synchronised BAMDP construct. A range of finite- and infinite-horizon task experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach in terms of both property satisfaction and sample efficiency, when compared to traditional model-free approaches. Additional ablation studies also successfully highlight the value of the novel BAMCP algorithm in comparison to classical BAMCP for LTL task satisfaction. Finally, we also showcase a successful application of our approach for \textit{cautious} RL, namely to reduce the number of task violations incurred during policy training.

</details>


### [149] [From Switching to Dynamic Regret: A Simple Reduction via Unbiased Random Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20968)
*Yibo Wang,Wenhao Yang,Sifan Yang,Yuanyu Wan,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个简洁的通用框架，将非平稳在线学习中的动态遗憾最小化归约为切换遗憾最小化。核心是构造一个对任意比较器序列都无偏、方差受控、切换次数可控的辅助随机序列，结合合适的替身损失将动态遗憾分解为期望切换遗憾与受控方差之和。由此对强凸和 exp-concave 损失建立了 ~O(T^{1/3} P_T^{2/3}) 的动态遗憾界，对一般凸损失恢复 O(√(T(1+P_T))) 的界，三者均达到极小极大最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非平稳在线学习场景中，动态遗憾是衡量在线学习器相对时变比较器序列性能的重要指标。尽管该领域已取得显著进展，但在强凸和 exp-concave 损失下获得最优遗憾界通常需要繁琐复杂的分析，缺乏统一而简洁的方法。

Method: 提出一个将动态遗憾最小化归约为切换遗憾最小化的简化框架。核心思想是为任意给定的比较器序列构造一个辅助随机序列，该序列在每一轮保持无偏性、方差受控且切换次数可控；结合适当的替身损失，可将动态遗憾分解为相对该随机序列的期望切换遗憾与其受控方差之和，从而可直接复用现成的具备切换遗憾保证的算法。

Result: 理论结果：对强凸和 exp-concave 损失，建立 ~O(T^{1/3} P_T^{2/3}) 的动态遗憾界；对一般凸损失，该归约恢复 O(√(T(1+P_T))) 的动态遗憾界，其中 T 为时间步数、P_T 为比较器序列的路径长度。所有结果均与相应损失类型下的极小极大最优结果匹配。

Conclusion: 该框架以统一、简洁的方式覆盖强凸、exp-concave 和一般凸三类损失并均达到极小极大最优，充分体现了框架的通用性与实用性，降低了以往获得最优动态遗憾界所需的分析复杂度。

Abstract: In non-stationary online learning, dynamic regret has attracted increasing attention as a measure of how well an online learner performs against a time-varying comparator sequence. Despite considerable advances, attaining optimal bounds for strongly convex and exp-concave losses often involves intricate analysis. In this paper, we present a \textit{simple} framework that reduces dynamic regret minimization to switching regret minimization. As a result, we can derive dynamic regret bounds by using off-the-shelf algorithms with switching regret guarantees. The key idea of our reduction is to construct, for \textit{any} comparator sequence, an auxiliary random sequence that is unbiased at each round, with the controlled variance and a manageable number of switches. Combining this construction with suitable surrogate losses, we can decompose dynamic regret into the expected switching regret against the random sequence and its controlled variance. Theoretically, for strongly convex and exp-concave losses, we establish the $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/3}P_T^{2/3})$ dynamic regret bounds, where $T$ denotes the time horizon and $P_T$ denotes the path-length of the comparator sequence. Moreover, for general convex losses, the same reduction also recovers the $O(\sqrt{T(1+P_T)})$ dynamic regret bound. Notably, all our findings match the minimax optimal results for these three types of losses, highlighting the versatility of our proposed framework.

</details>


### [150] [Generative inversion for early ranking of competing geologic interpretations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20978)
*Harun Ur Rashid,Daniel O'Malley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个工作流，利用文本到图像基础模型将竞争性地质解释转化为替代空间先验，通过水力水头观测数据对解释进行排序和评估。在合成基准（Johansen地层，925个测试案例）和实际案例（WIPP Culebra白云岩段）中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高后果地面下决策常在数据稀缺条件下进行，专家对同一地下系统可能有竞争性解释，但项目早期缺乏实用方法判断哪种解释最接近现实。现有方法依赖主观判断或密集数据，均不适用于早期勘探阶段。

Method: 工作流步骤：(1) 将竞争性地质解释转化为替代空间先验；(2) 使用文本到图像基础模型为每种解释生成1600个地质图像集合；(3) 单独训练的变分自编码器提供解释专属的潜在表示；(4) 监督逆网络将水头观测映射到潜在空间；(5) 冻结的解码器生成图像并映射为对数电导率场；(6) 稳态流动模拟预测水头，计算不匹配度并转换为高斯形式兼容性得分。

Result: 在基于Johansen地层的合成基准中，三种解释（Precise & Accurate、Accurate、Mismatched）的均方根误差分别从0.197增至0.227和0.280，与解释一致性递减顺序吻合。在WIPP Culebra白云岩段实际应用中，修订模型获得0.991的兼容性权重，远高于原始模型的0.009，与独立证据一致。

Conclusion: 该工作流能够在数据稀少条件下客观评估竞争性地质解释，将主观解释转化为可量化的兼容性排名，为早期地下工程决策提供科学依据。

Abstract: High-consequence subsurface decisions are often made under severe data scarcity. Experts may arrive at competing interpretations of the same subsurface system, yet early in a project there is rarely a practical way to determine which one is most realistic. This uncertainty can persist until several wells are drilled, often costing millions of dollars. Existing approaches for evaluating geologic interpretations rely either on subjective judgment or on dense data that are rarely available in early-stage investigations. We present a workflow that addresses this challenge by translating competing geologic interpretations into alternative spatial priors and ranking them according to their consistency with hydraulic-head observations. For each interpretation, a text-to-image foundation model generates an ensemble of 1600 geologic images, and a separately trained variational autoencoder provides an interpretation-specific latent representation. A supervised inverse network maps the head observations into this latent space, and the frozen decoder produces an image that is mapped to a log-conductivity field. Steady-state flow simulation then provides predicted heads, and the resulting mismatch is converted into a Gaussian-form compatibility score. We evaluate the framework using a synthetic benchmark based on the Johansen Formation and three interpretations of decreasing consistency with the reference representation. Across 925 test cases, the mean head RMSE increases from 0.197 for the Precise \& Accurate interpretation to 0.227 for the Accurate interpretation and 0.280 for the Mismatched interpretation. We subsequently apply the workflow to two published conceptual models of the Culebra Dolomite Member at the Waste Isolation Pilot Plant. The revised model receives a compatibility weight of 0.991, compared with 0.009 for the original model, consistent with the independent evidence.

</details>


### [151] [ASGARD: Action-Space Guard for UAV Resilience via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20982)
*Mohsen Salehi,Karthik Pattabiraman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ASGARD，一个两阶段师生流水线，使基于强化学习（RL）的无人机（UAV）控制系统对动作空间攻击具有鲁棒性。在教师阶段，一个编码器将无人机物理状态与动作攻击相关的特权信息相结合，产生动作攻击感知的潜变量，用于训练RL控制策略以及一个输出修正动作命令给执行器的监控器。在学生阶段，编码器和监控器通过监督学习从教师对应模块学习，仅使用无人机物理状态历史即可在机载运行。实验表明ASGARD在面对针对不同动作命令的攻击时能保持鲁棒性并完成任务，还能泛化到未见过的攻击，并对隐蔽攻击保持鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL控制器已广泛用于无人机导航与控制，但它们容易遭受动作空间攻击——即在策略生成动作命令之后、执行器执行之前覆盖动作命令。现有大多数防御针对策略输入的攻击，而那些针对动作空间攻击的防御会在训练时期重新训练策略，无法在运行时对已被污染的动作保持韧性，因此需要在运行时防御动作空间攻击的方法。

Method: ASGARD采用两阶段教师-学生流水线。教师阶段：编码器将无人机物理状态与动作攻击相关的特权信息结合，生成动作攻击感知的潜变量，用于训练RL控制策略以及一个监控器，该监控器向执行器输出修正后的动作命令。学生阶段：编码器和监控器均通过从教师对应模块的监督学习进行训练，从而仅利用无人机物理状态历史即可在机载运行。

Result: 在多种针对无人机不同动作命令的攻击场景下评估ASGARD，结果发现ASGARD对动作空间攻击具有韧性，尽管遭受攻击仍能完成任务；此外，ASGARD能泛化到未见过的攻击，并对隐蔽攻击（stealthy attacks）保持韧性。

Conclusion: ASGARD通过动作攻击感知的教师-学生流水线，成功使基于RL的无人机控制在面对运行时动作空间攻击时具备鲁棒性，能完成任务并在未见攻击及隐蔽攻击下保持韧性，为动作空间攻击的运行时防御提供了一种有效方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) controllers have been recently adopted for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAV) navigation and control. However, they are susceptible to action-space attacks that overwrite the action commands after the policy generates them and before the actuators execute them. While most existing defenses target attacks on the policy's inputs, those addressing action-space attacks retrain the policy at training time and are not resilient to corrupted actions at runtime. We propose ASGARD, a two-phase teacher-student pipeline for making RL-based UAV control resilient to action-space attacks. In the teacher phase, an encoder combines the UAV's physical state with action-attack-related privileged information to produce an action-attack-aware latent that trains the RL control policy and a monitor that outputs corrected action commands to the actuators. In the student phase, both the encoder and the monitor are trained via supervised learning from their teacher counterparts to run on-board using only the UAV's physical state history. We evaluate ASGARD across attack scenarios targeting different action commands on UAV. We find that ASGARD is resilient to action-space attacks and completes the missions despite the attack. We further find that ASGARD generalizes to unseen attacks and remains resilient against stealthy attacks.

</details>


### [152] [From Stress to Affect: Multimodal Deep Learning for Physiological Emotion Recognition Across Wearable Sensor Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20991)
*Desta Haileselassie Hagos,Saurav Keshari Aryal,Legand L. Burge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对基于可穿戴传感器的生理情绪识别进行了系统性比较研究，评估了双向LSTM、TCN和Transformer三种时间深度学习架构在WESAD和EmoWear两个多模态数据集上的表现，涵盖腕部、胸部及多模态感知配置，并采用留一受试者交叉验证、软投票集成、传感器消融、采样频率分析和梯度显著性等方法。结果显示Transformer在WESAD上取得最佳多模态准确率（99.02%），LSTM在EmoWear上对唤醒度和效价均表现最佳（分别91.80%和89.96%），表明架构性能取决于数据集特性而非某单一架构普遍最优；多模态感知始终优于单一配置，4 Hz采样频率在训练成本与性能间提供了实用平衡点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生理情绪识别研究通常只评估单一模型、感知配置或数据集，缺乏对这些关键因素如何影响识别性能的系统性理解，难以为实际应用中的架构选择、模态配置和采样频率提供可靠指导。

Method: 在WESAD和EmoWear两个多模态可穿戴数据集上，系统比较双向LSTM、TCN和Transformer三种时间深度学习架构，分别在腕部、胸部和多模态感知配置下使用留一受试者交叉验证进行评估，并进一步探究软投票集成、传感器消融、不同采样频率以及基于梯度的显著图分析。

Result: Transformer在WESAD多模态配置下达到最高准确率99.02%±0.51%；LSTM在EmoWear上对唤醒度（91.80%±1.06%）和效价（89.96%±0.36%）均取得最佳多模态结果。多模态感知在两个数据集上均优于腕部或胸部单一配置。采样频率分析显示4 Hz为实用工作点，在性能与训练成本之间达到良好平衡。

Conclusion: 架构的相对性能受数据集特性影响，不存在普遍最优的统一架构；实际部署时应优先考虑多模态感知配置，并可将采样频率降至4 Hz以降低计算成本。这些发现为可穿戴生理情绪识别系统的架构选择、模态配置和采样频率决策提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Physiological emotion recognition using wearable sensors has important applications in mental health monitoring, affective computing, and human-computer interaction. However, existing studies typically evaluate a single model, sensing configuration, or dataset, limiting our understanding of how these factors influence recognition performance. We present a comparative study of temporal deep learning architectures for physiological emotion recognition using two multimodal wearable datasets: WESAD and EmoWear. Bidirectional long short-term memory (LSTM), temporal convolutional network (TCN), and Transformer models are evaluated under wrist-only, chest-only, and multimodal sensing configurations using participant-independent leave-one-subject-out cross-validation (LOSO-CV). We also investigate soft-voting ensembles, sensor ablation, sampling frequency, and gradient-based saliency. The Transformer achieved the highest multimodal accuracy on WESAD (99.02% +/- 0.51%), whereas the LSTM achieved the best multimodal accuracy on EmoWear for both arousal (91.80% +/- 1.06%) and valence (89.96% +/- 0.36%). These results show that relative architecture performance depends on dataset characteristics rather than one architecture being uniformly superior. Multimodal sensing consistently outperformed wrist-only and chest-only configurations across both datasets. Sampling-frequency analysis showed that 4 Hz provides a practical operating point, with performance comparable to higher frequencies at substantially lower training cost. These findings provide guidance for selecting architectures, sensing modalities, and sampling frequencies for wearable physiological emotion recognition.

</details>


### [153] [MOSAIC-SR: Transformer-Guided Symbolic Regression for Scientific Equation Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20997)
*Peiyi Zheng,Yanming Kang,Hans De Sterck,Giang Tran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MOSAIC-SR，一种结合预训练 Transformer 与局部搜索的符号回归方法。它利用预训练模型生成多个初始草图来初始化多个搜索区域，避免在巨大表达式空间中随机启动，并通过尺度感知的常数优化与局部符号修复联合恢复结构与常数。在 SRSD-Feynman 数据集（含/不含哑变量）及六个额外基准上，MOSAIC-SR 在所有数据集上获得最高符号解率，预测精度位居前两名，且在存在无关哑输入时优势依然保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归方法难以兼顾灵活的结构搜索与高效的推理：基于搜索的方法虽能优化表达式结构，但常依赖昂贵且随机初始化的组合优化；预训练神经网络虽能近乎即时地生成公式，但其预测常包含符号错误。因此需要一种既能高效利用学习先验又能保证结构恢复精度的方法。

Method: MOSAIC-SR 采用预训练 Transformer 提出多个初始草图，用这些草图在若干有前景的区域初始化搜索，避免随机起点。每次搜索通过尺度感知的常数优化和局部符号修复，联合恢复表达式结构与常数。

Result: 在 SRSD-Feynman 数据集（含与不含哑变量）及六个额外基准上，MOSAIC-SR 在所有数据集上取得最高符号解率，预测精度排名前二；在存在无关哑输入时该优势依然保持。

Conclusion: 实验表明，学习先验可聚焦搜索于有前景的方程结构，且数值优化与符号修复对准确恢复公式至关重要。

Abstract: Symbolic regression aims to recover closed-form equations from observations, providing interpretable models for scientific discovery. Existing approaches struggle to combine flexible structural search with efficient inference. Search-based methods can refine expression structure but often rely on costly combinatorial optimization with random initialization. Pretrained neural models generate formulas almost instantly, but their predictions often contain symbolic errors. We introduce MOSAIC-SR, which uses a pretrained Transformer to propose multiple initial sketches. These sketches initialize searches in several promising regions, avoiding random starts in the vast expression space. Each search jointly recovers structure and constants through scale-aware constant optimization and local symbolic repair. We evaluate MOSAIC-SR on the SRSD-Feynman dataset with and without dummy variables and on six additional benchmarks. MOSAIC-SR obtains the highest symbolic solution rate on every dataset while ranking among the top two methods in predictive accuracy. This advantage persists in the presence of irrelevant dummy inputs. The results show that learned priors can focus search on promising equation structures, and that numerical optimization and symbolic repair are important for recovery.

</details>


### [154] [On the Limits of Maximal Coding Rate Reduction for Out-of-Distribution Generalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21001)
*Menghui Zhou,Gaoshan Bi,Vitaveska Lanfranchi,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了极大编码率降低（MCR²）框架在分布偏移下的泛化极限，证明其本身可完全失败于OOD泛化：基于不稳定环境特征的表征能获得全局编码最优却在下游预测上完全失灵；即使训练覆盖所有测试输入，编码质量仍可接近最优而预测误差接近100%。直接引入IRM/REx的不变性原则也不能消除这一失败，因为该失败表征在训练环境中共享同一最优编码算子。可靠的OOD保障需要额外的新假设或学习原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCR²是一个有前景的信息论框架，用于学习结构化、判别性的类子流形表征，并启发了可解释的白盒架构。但作者观察到MCR²在分布偏移下可能完全失败，因此需要系统研究其OOD泛化极限，以厘清基于编码率减少框架进行稳定预测的边界。

Method: 通过构造反例（counterexample）进行理论分析。作者构建了精确全局最优的编码表征实例：一类完全基于不稳定环境特征的表征能达到全局编码最优但相关性反转后完全失败，尽管存在可用且稳定的特征；同时构造了训练覆盖所有测试输入时，编码质量任意接近最优而预测错误任意接近100%的情形。并进一步检查IRM/REx不变性原则的直接融入是否可消除该失败。

Result: 表明MCR²目标单独存在两类OOD失败：一是精确全局最优时可出现完全预测失败（含训练中不会出现的测试输入）；二是即使所有可能的测试输入都出现在训练中，编码质量可任意接近最优而预测错误任意接近100%。此外，直接融入IRM/REx的不变性原则无法消除失败，失败表征在训练环境中共享相同的最优编码算子，说明共享编码最优性不能保证稳定预测。

Conclusion: MCR²无法仅凭自身或其与IRM/REx不变式原则的简单结合提供可靠的OOD泛化保证；需要新的假设或学习原则来建立跨环境的稳定预测关系。

Abstract: Substantial efforts have been devoted to making deep learning objectives, representations, and architectures interpretable, with the goal of improving the safety, robustness, and generalisation of learning systems in diverse real-world applications. The recently proposed maximal coding rate reduction ($\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$) offers a promising information-theoretic framework for learning structured, discriminative representations of class-wise submanifolds and has inspired interpretable white-box architectures. However, we observe that $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ can completely fail under distribution shift, motivating our study of its out-of-distribution (OOD) generalisation limits. We establish two limitations of $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ for OOD generalisation. First, the $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ objective alone can admit complete prediction failure: a representation based entirely on unstable environmental features can achieve the global coding optimum yet fail completely after correlation reversal, despite an available perfectly stable feature. This exact-optimum example includes test inputs that cannot occur during training. Even when every possible test input can also occur during training, coding quality can be arbitrarily close to optimal while prediction error is arbitrarily close to 100%. Second, directly incorporating the invariance principle underlying widely successful invariant risk minimisation (IRM) and risk extrapolation (REx) does not eliminate this failure. The failing representation admits the same optimal coding operator across training environments, showing that shared coding optimality does not ensure stable prediction. Reliable OOD guarantees for $\mathrm{MCR}^{2}$ therefore require additional new assumptions or learning principles that establish stable predictive relationships across environments.

</details>


### [155] [Scaling Discovery through Test-Time Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21032)
*Jongho Park,Vasilis Kontonis,Shivam Garg,Akshay Krishnamurthy,Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在推理时进行多智能体通信能在困难任务上显著超越独立并行尝试。共享一个突破性进展可推动整个团队前进。k个通信智能体（team@k）的成功率相当于4k个独立智能体，且优势随k增大而放大。在ARC-AGI-3、多联骨牌装箱、MNIST分类器压缩等任务上均取得超越现有最优的成绩（MNIST上以1957字节、99.4%准确率超过最佳人类方案）。但增益有条件：算力不足或缺乏清晰进展度量时，独立智能体可能更优；在充足算力和清晰反馈下，多智能体通信始终更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学进步依赖于协作而非孤立研究，但现有智能体系统很少具备协作能力。关于通信智能体是否有助益的结论此前存在争议（结果混杂）。作者旨在回答这一问题，探究测试时通信能否系统性地提升多智能体在困难问题求解上的表现。

Method: 在ARC-AGI-3基准上研究多智能体测试时通信，智能体无预定义角色，通过共享目录进行通信；通过缩放通信的智能体数量考察扩展规律。并将该机制迁移至研究导向任务（多联骨牌装箱、MNIST分类器压缩），同时对比受限算力与缺乏进展度量的情形下的表现。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-3上，k个通信智能体（team@k）的成功率匹配4k个独立智能体，且优势随k增长而扩大；单个智能体均无法解决的任务可通过团队可靠解决。多联骨牌装箱超越best@k及先前最优分数；MNIST压缩上通信方案超越最佳人类方案（4个智能体团队产出1957字节分类器，达99.4%测试准确率）。但增益非无条件：算力受限或无清晰进展度量时独立智能体可能更优。

Conclusion: 在充足算力与清晰反馈条件下，多智能体通信能持续产生更强的结果，其增益随规模扩大而复合增长，且可迁移至研究型任务。然而该优势依赖于算力充足与明确进展反馈这两个条件，并非普遍成立。

Abstract: Science advances not in isolation but through collaboration, yet existing agentic systems capture little of this. Whether communicating agents help remains an open question with mixed prior results. We show that test-time communication can substantially outperform independent parallel attempts on challenging tasks, where sharing a breakthrough can push the whole group forward. We first study the effect of scaling multi-agent test-time communication, where agents have no predefined roles and communicate via a shared directory, on ARC-AGI-3, a benchmark requiring novel problem solving. We find that a team of $k$ communicating agents, team@$k$, matches the success rate of $4k$ independent agents, and this advantage grows with $k$, suggesting gains compound with scale. The effect is not merely efficiency: a task that no single agent can solve, a team of agents can solve reliably. Furthermore, these gains transfer to research-oriented tasks, given sufficient compute. On polyomino packing, communicating agents outperform best@$k$ and exceed the prior best-known score. On MNIST classifier compression, communication surpasses the best-known human solution. A team of four agents produced a 1,957-byte classifier submission achieving 99.4% test accuracy, smaller than both the best-known human solution and the best single-agent result. These gains are not unconditional. Independent agents may outperform communication when compute is limited or when a clear measure of progress is absent. However, under sufficient compute and clear feedback, multi-agent communication consistently yields stronger results.

</details>


### [156] [Stiefel-AdamW: Geometry-Aware AdamW for Linear Factorization Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21039)
*Emanuele Zangrando,Marco Sutti,Francesco Tudisco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Stiefel-AdamW，一种针对深度学习中线因子分解块（如LoRA适配器、低秩压缩层、自注意力QK乘积）的优化器。通过将一个因子约束在Stiefel流形上、另一个保持欧几里得，该方法在保留AdamW坐标级对角预条件优势的同时，消除了GL(R^r)规范对称性导致的因子爆炸问题。在GPT2、ViT、Mistral 7B的LoRA微调和GPT2预训练中验证了优于AdamW的性能，且额外开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习中的线性因子分解块（W=BA）普遍存在非唯一分解的病态问题，这会破坏训练稳定性并限制可用的学习率。然而现有方法通常忽略其底层黎曼几何，使用标准欧几里得优化器（如AdamW）进行优化。

Method: Stiefel-AdamW将因子分解中的一个因子约束在Stiefel流形上，另一个保持欧几里得，从而将完整的GL(R^r)规范对称性松弛为紧致的正交对称性，防止因子爆炸。动量估计在环境欧几里得空间中完成，几何仅通过切空间投影和流形回缩引入，实现开销极小，同时保持AdamW的对角预条件特性并具备标准收敛保证。

Result: 在GPT2、ViT和Mistral 7B的LoRA风格微调，以及GPT2在OpenWebText上的完整预训练中，Stiefel-AdamW相比强基线（包括标准AdamW）均表现出一致的改进，且额外计算成本几乎可以忽略。

Conclusion: Stiefel-AdamW是一个近即插即用的AdamW替代方案，适用于任何包含线因子分解块的场景，兼顾了黎曼方法的稳定性和AdamW的实际优势，在不增加显著成本的情况下提升了训练效果。

Abstract: A pervasive structural pattern in modern deep learning is the linear factorization block: a submodule of the form $W = BA$ in which two parameter matrices are multiplied directly, with no intervening nonlinearity. Such blocks appear in LoRA adapters, low-rank compressed layers, query-key products of self-attention, and share a common pathology: the factorization is non-unique, which can destabilize training and limit usable learning rates. Despite this, factorization blocks are typically optimized with standard Euclidean methods that ignore the underlying geometry. We introduce Stiefel-AdamW, a near drop-in replacement for AdamW for use wherever such blocks appear. By constraining one factor on the Stiefel manifold while leaving the other Euclidean, Stiefel-AdamW relaxes the full $\mathrm{GL}(\mathbb{R}^r)$ gauge symmetry to a compact orthogonal symmetry, ruling out factor blow-up while retaining the coordinate-wise diagonal preconditioning that gives AdamW its practical strength. Moment estimation is performed in the ambient Euclidean space, with geometry entering only through a tangent-space projection and a manifold retraction. The implementation overhead over AdamW is minimal, and we show that the resulting optimizer inherits both the stability benefits of Riemannian methods and standard convergence guarantees. We validate Stiefel-AdamW on LoRA-style fine-tuning of GPT2, ViT, and Mistral 7B and on full pretraining of GPT2 on OpenWebText, showing consistent improvements over strong baselines at essentially no additional cost over AdamW.

</details>


### [157] [A Lightweight Plug-in Gate for Transformer-Based Time-Series Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21044)
*Hongkai Zhuang,Tao Huang,Chen Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间序列预测中协变量进入编码器之前的"输入侧接口"——预编码器协变量准入门控机制。该接口通过轻量级的表示级门控（sigmoid分数）来调节协变量表示，并提出了带使用正则化的变体。作为即插即用模块应用于TimeXer、iTransformer和PatchTST，在零额外调参协议下进行实验评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Transformer类预测器通常直接将协变量表示送入编码器，缺乏显式的准入（admission）阶段来筛选哪些外部变量及其表示应该进入目标预测路径，因此需要研究一个显式的输入侧接口来规范化这一过程。

Method: 提出预编码器协变量准入接口：1）一个轻量级的表示级门控模块，为表示单元分配sigmoid分数；2）带使用正则化的变体，惩罚平均准入分数。该接口作为插件模块应用于TimeXer、iTransformer和PatchTST，采用零额外调参协议（继承基线配置）。

Result: 在ETTm1、ETTm2、Traffic、Energy和ILI数据集上的实验中，门控模型与对应基线性能相当；使用惩罚降低了平均准入分数，同时预测误差保持在与无惩罚TimeXer设置接近的水平。

Conclusion: 预编码器协变量准入门控作为一种输入侧接口，能够在不进行额外调参的情况下与强基线竞争，且使用正则化能在保持预测精度的同时减少协变量准入，为协变量丰富场景下的时间序列预测提供了新的输入调控思路。

Abstract: Covariate-rich time-series forecasting requires deciding how external variables enter the target forecasting path. Existing Transformer-based forecasters usually build a covariate representation and pass it to the encoder without an explicit admission stage. This paper studies pre-encoder covariate admission as an input-side interface that regulates that representation immediately before encoder processing. We implement the interface with a lightweight representation-level pre-encoder gate that assigns sigmoid scores to representation units, and we also study a usage-regularized variant that penalizes average admission. The interface is evaluated as a plug-in module for TimeXer, Inverted Transformer (iTransformer), and Patch Time Series Transformer (PatchTST) under a zero-extra-tuning protocol, where each gated model inherits the corresponding baseline configuration. Experiments on the Electricity Transformer Temperature minute-level (ETTm1 and ETTm2) datasets, Traffic, Energy, and influenza-like illness (ILI) include paired forecasting comparisons, gate-placement ablation, initialization ablation, controlled covariate-admission analysis, and a variance inflation factor (VIF)-informed permutation feature importance (PFI) diagnostic case study. In the tested settings, the gate is competitive with the corresponding baselines, and the usage penalty reduces average admission scores while keeping forecasting errors close to the unpenalized TimeXer setting.

</details>


### [158] [FedeRage: Provably Convergent Agnostic Federated Learning under General Client Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21057)
*Herlock Rahimi,Dionysis Kalogerias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习在非独立同分布数据与未知客户端参与概率下的优化问题。作者首先刻画了分布不可知的FedAvg在参与情况完全未知时实际最小化的随机目标函数，然后提出FedeRage——一种基于条件风险价值（CVaR）的风险厌恶联邦平均算法。该算法通过在局部目标中嵌入CVaR来实现分布鲁棒优化，隐式地提高高损失和低频参与客户端的权重，仅需为每个客户端增加一个标量参数，理论收敛率为O(κ/√T)，并在多个异构基准上取得优于现有方法的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在非IID数据与随机客户端参与下性能退化，而现有基于FedAvg的改进方法大多假设服务器已知客户端参与概率，这在真实部署系统中几乎不可能。作者旨在解决参与概率完全未知（包括高度偏斜、每轮参与规模可变）情况下FedAvg的优化行为，并提出无需利用参与分布信息的鲁棒算法。

Method: 首先从理论上刻画分布不可知FedAvg在未知参与概率下实际求解的随机目标——均匀聚合最小化由参与诱导的边缘分布加权的随机目标，并给出凸非光滑情形下O(1/√T)的收敛率。在此基础上提出FedeRage算法：将条件风险价值（CVaR）嵌入每个客户端的局部目标函数，在自然分布鲁棒优化（DRO）框架下实现风险厌恶的联邦平均，隐式提高高损失与低频参与客户端的权重；该算法仅需为每个客户端维护一个标量参数，收敛率为O(κ/√T)，其中κ为风险厌恶代价的上界。

Result: 在三个异构基准数据集上的实验表明，FedeRage在准确性、公平性和收敛速度方面均一致优于现有最先进方法。与基于最优传输的聚合对齐方案相比，FedeRage无需知道可用性分布作为输入，保持了对参与分布的不可知性。

Conclusion: 本文为未知参与概率下的联邦学习提供了理论刻画与实用算法。FedeRage通过CVaR嵌入实现分布鲁棒的FedAvg扩展，在不依赖参与分布先验信息的前提下，同时提高了准确率、公平性和收敛效率，为实际部署中应对客户端参与不确定性提供了一种优雅且高效的方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without sharing raw data, but its performance degrades under non-IID data and stochastic client participation. Remedies built on classical Federated Averaging (FedAvg) typically presuppose that client participation probabilities are known to the server, which is rarely the case in deployed systems. We first discuss and then characterize the optimization problem that \emph{distributionally agnostic} FedAvg actually solves when participation is entirely unknown, possibly highly skewed, and of variable size across rounds: uniform aggregation is shown to minimize a well-defined stochastic objective, weighted by the participation-induced marginal, at a standard $\mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T})$ rate for convex and possibly nonsmooth losses. Building on this characterization, we propose \emph{Federated Risk-Averse Averaging} (\textsc{FedeRage}), a risk-averse extension of FedAvg that embeds the \emph{Conditional Value-at-Risk} (CVaR) into the local objective within a natural distributionally robust optimization (DRO) framework. \textsc{FedeRage} implicitly upweights high-loss and infrequently participating clients while adding only a \emph{single scalar per-client}, and admits an $\mathcal{O}(κ/\sqrt{T})$ rate in which the factor $κ$ is the upper bound on the ``price" of risk aversion. In contrast with aggregation-alignment schemes based on optimal transport, which require the availability distribution as an input, \textsc{FedeRage} remains agnostic to it. Several experiments on three heterogeneous benchmarks indicate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art methods in accuracy, fairness, and convergence speed.

</details>


### [159] [Toward individual-level calibration in affect recognition with perceptual adjustment queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21073)
*Xuanzhou Chen,Sankaraleengam Alagapan,Ashwin Pananjady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于感知调整查询（PAQ）的框架，用于对情绪面孔识别任务进行个体化感知难度归一化。通过直接估计每位被试在面部表情频谱上的最小可觉差（JND），重新表达刺激距离，构建难度等价的感知空间任务。在二择一强迫选择（2AFC）任务中，与未校准基线和群体级Weibull校准相比，PAQ校准显著均衡了个体层面的感知任务难度，同时降低了平均反应时间和被试间反应时间方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统面部情绪感知行为任务隐含假设：相同刺激对所有被试施加了等价的感知难度。然而这一假设被个体感知敏感性的系统性差异所违背，导致任务无法公平反映个体真实感知能力。

Method: 以情绪感知任务为测试平台，提出通过认知轻量的感知调整查询（PAQ）直接估计每位被试沿面部情绪频谱的JND，利用JND重新标定刺激距离，构建感知空间中难度均衡的任务，并通过二元元认知难度判断和反应时方差分解两种互补行为指标在2AFC任务中验证。

Result: PAQ校准在个体层面显著均衡了感知任务难度，优于未校准基线和群体级Weibull校准；同时降低了平均反应时间及被试间反应时方差。

Conclusion: PAQ可作为面部情绪识别中一种原则性且实用的个体化感知校准工具，为消除个体感知敏感性差异对任务公平性的影响提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Behavioral tasks measuring facial affect perception assume that identical stimuli impose equivalent perceptual difficulty across participants. However, this assumption is systematically violated by individual differences in perceptual sensitivity. Using an affective perception task as our testbed, we propose a framework to normalize for perceptual difficulty that directly estimates each participant's Just Noticeable Difference (JND) along the facial affect spectrum via cognitively lightweight perceptual adjustment queries (PAQs). We use these PAQ-inferred JNDs to re-express stimulus distances, constructing difficulty-equated tasks in perceptual space. We validate the framework in a Two-Alternative Forced-Choice (2AFC) task using two complementary behavioral measures: binary metacognitive difficulty judgments and response time variance decomposition. We find that PAQ calibration significantly equalizes perceived task difficulty at an individual level when compared to both the non-calibrated baseline and population-level Weibull calibration, while also reducing mean response time and between-subject variance in response time. These results establish PAQ as a principled and practical instrument for individualized perceptual calibration in facial affect recognition.

</details>


### [160] [REFINEPPO: Learning Continuous Control Policies by Iterative Action Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21108)
*Sachini Weerasekara,Sagar Kamarthi,Jacqueline Isaacs*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代动作细化（IAR）方法，将深度强化学习中的策略从"一步前馈预测动作"改为"通过一系列学习到的残差修正逐步构建动作"，并结合PPO形成REFINEPPO算法。在14个基准控制任务上，REFINEPPO达到或超过标准PPO的性能，并在多个任务上实现更快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续控制策略通常被定义为从观测状态到动作（或动作分布）的直接映射，仅需一次前馈网络即可生成最优控制决策。这种形式化虽然有效，但策略一旦形成初始预测后几乎没有机会重新考虑或逐步改进动作。作者质疑：与其只学习直接预测动作，能否让策略学会迭代改进动作，以及这种迭代过程能否在策略学习阶段带来优势。

Method: 提出迭代动作细化（IAR）方法：通过一系列学习到的残差修正来构建控制动作。具体包括：(1) 从初始提议开始，共享细化网络反复以观测状态和当前动作提议为条件，每步细化都修正前几步构造的动作；(2) 最终细化提议决定智能体执行的动作；(3) 将该迭代动作构建机制与PPO集成，形成REFINEPPO算法。

Result: 在14个基准控制任务上评估REFINEPPO，并辅以细化深度与更新计划的受控消融实验，以及对迭代细化为何有效的分析。结果显示REFINEPPO在多数环境中达到或超过标准PPO的性能，并在多个任务上展现出更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: 迭代式动作构建为连续控制策略提供了一种优于直接单次预测的替代方案，能够在不牺牲最终性能的前提下提升学习效率与收敛速度，为深度强化学习策略网络的设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) has achieved strong performance across a wide range of continuous-control problems. These continuous-control policies, however, are often defined as direct mappings from an observed state to an action or action distribution, requiring a single feed-forward network to construct an optimal control decision in one pass. While effective, this formulation leaves little opportunity for the policy to reconsider or progressively improve an action once an initial prediction has been formed. In this work, we explore an alternative approach: rather than learning only to directly predict an action, can a policy learn to iteratively improve one, and can this iterative process provide advantages during policy learning? We introduce Iterative Action Refinement (IAR), an iterative action-construction method that constructs control actions through a sequence of learned residual corrections. Starting from an initial proposal, a shared refinement network repeatedly conditions on the observed state and the current action proposal, allowing each refinement step to revise the action constructed by preceding steps. The final refined proposal is then used to determine the action executed by the agent. We integrate this iterative action-construction mechanism with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO), yielding REFINEPPO. We evaluate REFINEPPO across 14 benchmark control tasks, complemented by controlled ablations of refinement depth and update schedules and analyses aimed at understanding why iterative refinement is effective. Across these environments, REFINEPPO matches or exceeds the performance of standard PPO while demonstrating faster convergence on several tasks.

</details>


### [161] [Talk to Me, Jarvis: An Open-Source Edge-Deployable Voice Assistant Framework for Autonomous Racecars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21109)
*Daniel Henel,Frederik Werner,Alexander Langmann,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Jarvis，一个面向自动驾驶车辆高层行为指令的离线语音助手系统。该系统将语音识别、语音合成与自然语言命令分类集成到轻量级本地框架中，核心是基于Mistral 7B领域微调构建的文本到命令分类器。实验表明其意图识别准确率达97.63%，平均处理延迟1.39秒，性能超越大规模在线托管模型，适合快速响应场景，并提供开源实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型作为语音助手后端组件在意图理解和上下文感知输入分类方面效果提升，但在线托管模型存在网络依赖和推理延迟不稳定的问题，限制了其在时间敏感的自动驾驶应用中的适用性。因此需要开发离线方案以解决上述瓶颈。

Method: 开发Jarvis离线语音助手，架构集成语音识别与语音合成，并融合自然语言命令分类到轻量级本地框架。核心组件为文本到命令分类器，采用Mistral 7B模型进行领域特定微调，实现低延迟推理。同时提供开源实现以支持进一步研究。

Result: 实验评估表明，该方案优于更大规模的在线托管模型，实现97.63%的意图识别准确率，平均处理延迟仅1.39秒，非常适合需要快速响应的应用场景。

Conclusion: Jarvis通过离线轻量级架构与领域微调的Mistral 7B分类器，在保证高意图识别准确率的同时显著降低延迟，验证了其在自动驾驶时间关键型任务中的适用性，并开源实现以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have improved their effectiveness as back-end components for voice assistants, particularly in intent understanding and context-aware input classification. However, online-hosted models introduce network dependency and variable inference latency, limiting their suitability for time-critical autonomous driving applications. In this work, we address these issues by developing Jarvis, an offline voice assistant for high-level behavioral commands of autonomous vehicles. Its architecture integrates speech recognition and synthesis with natural language command classification into a lightweight, local framework. Jarvis core component is a text-to-command classifier, built using a domain-specific fine-tuning of the Mistral 7B model, demonstrating low-latency inference. Our experimental evaluation demonstrates that our solution outperforms larger online-hosted models, achieving 97.63 % intent recognition accuracy with an average processing latency of 1.39 s, making it well-suited for operations requiring quick response times. To support further research and fine-tuning, we provide an open-source implementation.

</details>


### [162] [Signal-Centric Remote Sensing via Alternative Preprocessing and Acoustic Processing for ML-Driven Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.21123)
*Logan Luna,Sirio Jansen-Sánchez,Ilteris Demirkiran,Leo Ghelarducci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种替代传统基于图像的声纳数据处理方法，采用CSV格式数据进行处理，在自主系统上实现了91.18%的处理时间缩减，同时提升了机器学习目标检测精度以及SNR、PSNR等评价指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统声纳数据处理主要依赖图像表示，需要在自主系统上进行图像数据预处理，这带来了较高的计算开销和时延。作者旨在寻找一种更高效的数据处理方式，以适合资源受限的自主系统远程传感应用。
